Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on sorting structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 186. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 263 with relations in degree 1.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on sorting structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 186. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 263. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on sorting structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 121. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 624 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 121 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the sorting structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 624. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
